| Akito | Hi.<br>Is Len around? | talk |
| MEIKO | Hello, Akito, Toya.<br>Len hasn't shown up, yet. | talk |
| MEIKO | You wanted to talk to him? | talk |
| Toya | He said he'd DJ for us here today. | talk |
| MEIKO | In that case he should be here any moment. Take a seat and I'll make you coffee. | talk |
| Toya | Thank you, MEIKO.<br>Akito, let's sit by the window. | talk |
| Akito | Again?<br>That must be your favorite table. | talk |
| Toya | It gives me the best view of the cafe. | talk |
| Akito | So that's why. I like the ambiance, too.<br>Reminds me of Weekend Garage. | talk |
| MEIKO | That's probably because you guys share an attachment to that place. | talk |
| MEIKO | Here's your coffee. | talk |
| Akito | Thanks. | talk |
| MEIKO | Did you notice the RAD WEEKEND flyer outside? | talk |
| Akito | I didn't until now! | talk |
| MEIKO | That's there as a reminder of your wish to surpass it. | talk |
| Toya | Our feelings and ambitions manifest in a variety of ways throughout this world, I suppose. | talk |
| Akito | It feels so eerie, though, that a weird place like this exists, and that we can just chill here sipping coffee. | talk |
| Toya | You think it's weird? | talk |
| Akito | Well, if this isn't weird then I don't know what is. | talk |
| Akito | Like, where even are we, exactly?<br>How come we get teleported here by playing a song on a phone? | talk |
| Toya | It's a world created by our feelings, so the song which encompasses them is our link to it. | talk |
| Akito | I know that, but that's not the point. | talk |
| Toya | Well, I don't know any more about it than you do... | talk |
| Toya | But I just accept that it is what it is. | talk |
| Akito | You just accept it? | talk |
| Toya | It's not that I haven't wondered about it too. | talk |
| Akito | Really? Because despite always looking so serious and diligent, you've got that weird tendency to totally ignore anything that might be a pain... | talk |
| Toya | How to explain it...<br>To me, the inner workings of this world don't really matter. | talk |
| Toya | It's a SEKAI created from our feelings.<br>I like it here. And to me, that's enough. | talk |
| Akito | Okay, but it keeps bugging the heck out of me. | talk |
| MEIKO | Sorry to interrupt your lively chat.<br>More coffee? | talk |
| Akito | Yes, please.<br>Toya, aren't you into crime novels? | talk |
| Akito | Isn't part of the fun of reading those trying to figure out who's got an alibi, what the murder weapon was, and the like? So why aren't you curious about this world? | talk |
| Toya | In those stories, there's usually some crafty trick involved, but I don't think that's the case here. | talk |
| Akito | How can you be so sure? | talk |
| MEIKO | Hehe... | talk |
|  |  | Sound |
| Toya | Are you laughing at us? | talk |
| MEIKO | Of course! You two are just like Rin and Len! | talk |
| Akito | Really? | talk |
| Akito | I don't know if that's something to be proud about or insulted by... | talk |
| MEIKO | I don't see anything wrong with it. | talk |
|  |  | Sound |
| MEIKO | Sometimes Rin and Len have wild fights, but they respect each other despite that. | talk |
| MEIKO | Each insists it's them that's right, so it's hard work getting them to apologize and make up... | talk |
| MEIKO | But I think they're only able to argue their points so passionately because they trust each. | talk |
| Toya | I suppose trust plays into this. | talk |
| Toya | But it takes more than trust to be able to defend your opinion even if you know it will upset your friend. | talk |
| MEIKO | You think so? | talk |
| Toya | Fighting with your friends doesn't feel good.<br>It hurts to feel misunderstood by them. | talk |
| Toya | That's why I think the setting matters, too. It needs to be somewhere you feel comfortable and safe, otherwise the stress of the situation would be too much. | talk |
| Toya | A place like this cafe, or like Weekend Garage, gives you the reassurance you need for that. | talk |
| MEIKO | Hehe. Thanks, Toya.<br>I am happy to hear that as the owner of this cafe. | talk |
| Akito | Yeah, true. | talk |
| Akito | Maybe it's not just this cafe, but this entire place that offers a safe and accepting environment to us. | talk |
| MEIKO | I don't see why not! SEKAI exists for you to be yourselves and breathe easy. | talk |
| MEIKO | So take advantage of that and keep dropping in for my coffee! | talk |

| Miku | You look like you're having fun, MEIKO. | talk |
| MEIKO | Oh, hello, Miku.<br>Welcome to the cafe. | talk |
| MEIKO | I don't remember asking for any help today, so are you here as a customer? | talk |
| Miku | That's right. But you're in the midst of something, right? Anything I can do to help? | talk |
| MEIKO | You want to help?<br>That'd be great. | talk |
| Miku | So... What're you doing, anyway? | talk |
| MEIKO | I'm sorting out the coffee beans while there's still no one in the cafe. | talk |
| Miku | Why? | talk |
| MEIKO | There will always be a few beans that are not in the best of conditions when you buy them from a shop. | talk |
| MEIKO | Keeping these damaged beans will affect the taste of the coffee that you brew. | talk |
| Miku | Wow, you really know a lot. | talk |
| MEIKO | It was written on the label of the bag of beans I bought. Wasn't it nice of them to warn us? | talk |
| Miku | Oh, you just read what was on the label... | talk |
| Miku | How do you pick out the damaged beans, though? | talk |
| MEIKO | You spread the beans out like this and split them up into usable and unusable beans. | talk |
| Miku | But there are so many beans! | talk |
| MEIKO | It's not as hard as it looks. For example, see this whitish bean here? That's not usable. Easy to spot, right? | talk |
| Miku | You're right. The color's completely different. | talk |
| MEIKO | See? And then there's the opposite—overly dark beans. | talk |
| Miku | That's not usable too... It's really quite easy to tell if you look closely enough. | talk |
| MEIKO | Hehe. So, can you help me with this? | talk |
| Miku | Alright... | talk |
| MEIKO | Let's see... Can you pick out the white beans and beans that are of a different size than usual? | talk |
| Miku | Yeah, I can do that. | talk |
| MEIKO | Thank you.<br>Let's make delicious coffee together! | talk |
| Miku | ... | talk |
| MEIKO | ... | talk |
| Miku | ... | talk |
| MEIKO | ... | talk |
| Miku | MEIKO... | talk |
| MEIKO | Mhm? | talk |
| Miku | This is really boring work, huh? | talk |
| MEIKO | Hehe, you could say that again. | talk |
| MEIKO | But there's a big difference between the coffee brewed from before and after doing this. | talk |
| Miku | Is it really that big? | talk |
| MEIKO | Yes. And don't you want these unroasted beans to become a delicious pot of coffee? Especially since it would only take a little effort on your part? | talk |
| MEIKO | I would rather have something more delicious than not if I can help it. | talk |
| Miku | You too? | talk |
| MEIKO | Yes, of course. | talk |
| MEIKO | I also make coffee for myself, you know.<br>Not just for customers. | talk |
